**[00:20:52.380] DICT Assistant Secretary Pinky T. Jimenez** (asr-000208)

**[00:20:58.340] DICT Assistant Secretary Pinky T. Jimenez** (asr-000209)

**[00:21:03.360] DICT Assistant Secretary Pinky T. Jimenez** (asr-000210)  
In 2025, the ICT laid 3,026 kilometers of fiber optics, activated 59 nodes, and connected 1,686 government agencies

**[00:21:15.920] DICT Assistant Secretary Pinky T. Jimenez** (asr-000211)  
with estimated 26.47 million members of transacting public service by existing NBP and GovNet infrastructure.

**[00:21:28.340] DICT Assistant Secretary Pinky T. Jimenez** (asr-000212)

**[00:21:38.660] DICT Assistant Secretary Pinky T. Jimenez** (asr-000213)  
and the existing of the five GovNet.

**[00:21:42.580] DICT Assistant Secretary Pinky T. Jimenez** (asr-000214)

**[00:21:51.660] DICT Assistant Secretary Pinky T. Jimenez** (asr-000215)  
We continue for the backbone maintenance and sustain a large GovNet footprint.

**[00:21:58.340] DICT Assistant Secretary Pinky T. Jimenez** (asr-000216)

**[00:22:14.180] DICT Assistant Secretary Pinky T. Jimenez** (asr-000217)

**[00:22:26.880] DICT Assistant Secretary Pinky T. Jimenez** (asr-000219)

**[00:22:40.880] DICT Assistant Secretary Pinky T. Jimenez** (asr-000222)

of 10,259

**[00:32:32.580] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000502)  
Madam Chair, with your permission, if I may explain the next project, DICT projects.

**[00:32:39.380] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000503)  
So, the government connectivity must also remain available during disasters and emergencies.

**[00:32:45.700] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000504)  
So, that is the role of the government emergency communication system.

**[00:32:48.940] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000505)  
So, in 2025, the DICT deployed 76 LEO satellites or Low Earth Orbit Satellites sets, operated 10 mobile operations vehicles, and served about 750,000 beneficiaries of MOVE sets and established 97 emergency communication sites.

**[00:33:08.800] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000506)  
So, for 2026, the program targets to deploy 93 LEO Low Earth Orbit Satellite sets and 13 MOVE sets.

**[00:33:17.960] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000507)  
With one...

**[00:33:18.940] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000508)  
1.2 million beneficiaries.

**[00:33:20.940] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000509)  
So, one early warning broadcast system and 20 network-in-a-box units.

**[00:33:25.500] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000510)  
So, as of August 31, 76 satellite sets and 10 MOVE sets have been deployed.

**[00:33:30.400] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000511)  
So, beneficiaries, we have reached 1.26 million already, exceeding the annual target.

**[00:33:39.220] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000512)  
And all 20 network-in-a-box units have been delivered.

**[00:33:43.160] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000513)  
So, the early warning broadcast system remains under procurement.

**[00:33:46.560] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000514)  
So, for fiscal year 2027.

**[00:33:48.940] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000515)  
The 310.5 million NEP supports 68 Low Earth Orbit Satellite sets and additional 3 MOVE sets.

**[00:33:57.780] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000516)  
And the 1.3...

**[00:33:59.740] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000517)  
Covering 1.3 million beneficiaries and operated...

**[00:34:03.660] DICT Assistant Secretary Christian Mark G. Guingcangco** (asr-000518)  
And the continued operation of the early warning broadcast system and network-in-a-box capabilities.

**[01:00:09.740] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-001192)

**[01:00:23.460] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-001195)

**[01:00:28.460] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-001196)

**[01:00:41.480] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-001197)  
The e-goop super app, we already have more than 60 million users now.

**[01:00:45.640] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-001198)

**[01:00:48.980] DICT Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.** (asr-001199)

**[01:29:19.220] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001928)

**[01:29:32.980] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001935)

**[01:29:41.120] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001939)

**[01:29:43.000] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001940)  
these will be continuing programs in

**[01:29:44.780] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001941)  
2027, Madam Chair, but

**[01:29:46.860] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001942)  
the modest increase of 60 million

**[01:29:48.960] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001943)  
for 2027 will be

**[01:29:51.080] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001944)  
used to further fund

**[01:29:52.440] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001945)  
our strategic priority programs

**[01:29:55.000] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001946)  
for next year. This would include

**[01:29:57.240] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001947)  
enhancement of our

**[01:29:58.820] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001948)  
enhanced security response

**[01:30:00.000] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001949)  
and Investigation Task Force project, specifically increasing the number of dark websites being monitored by the NPC

**[01:30:08.340] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001950)  
for chatter or information on data breaches.

**[01:30:13.940] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001951)  
So part of the ongoing programs of the NPC is our breach notification.

**[01:30:18.540] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001952)  
So those entities that have experienced data breaches are supposed to notify the NPC within 72 hours of the discovery of the breach.

**[01:30:27.340] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001953)  
But we also have programs to be proactive in terms of identifying breaches even before they are reported to us.

**[01:30:35.520] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001954)  
And this bump in the budget in 2027 will further expand that proactive capability of the NPC.

**[01:30:42.680] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001955)  
It will also allow us to leverage AI technology with respect to our complaints management system.

**[01:30:49.800] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001956)  
So one of the priority projects next year is to have an AI chatbot for NPC

**[01:30:54.660] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001957)  
so that instead of reporting,

**[01:30:57.340] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001958)  
responding to queries or inquiries through email or telephone or walk-in,

**[01:31:02.880] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001959)  
stakeholders across the nation can get responses in real-time

**[01:31:06.820] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001960)  
through a chatbot to be developed next year using the additional budget.

**[01:31:11.760] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001961)  
The additional budget will also enhance our data management and process analytics digital platform or DAMPA,

**[01:31:18.980] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001962)  
specifically the component on our registration.

**[01:31:22.260] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001963)  
So it will increase the capacity of the system to process registration,

**[01:31:27.340] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001964)  
registrations at any one time.

**[01:31:30.000] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001965)  
So currently it can process around 1,000 applications at the same time.

**[01:31:34.480] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001966)  
But since we have priority programs to further expand the number of registered entities across the nation,

**[01:31:42.180] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001967)  
we find it necessary to first increase the capability of the system before we roll out the program.

**[01:31:47.740] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001968)  
Part of the enhancement will also allow us to reduce our turnaround time

**[01:31:52.680] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001969)  
in terms of processing these registration applications

**[01:31:56.620] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001970)  
from an average

**[01:31:57.320] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001971)  
of 7 days to an average of 3 days.

**[01:32:00.820] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001972)  
Next slide, please.

**[01:32:05.320] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001973)  
Let me move to the next slide.

**[01:32:08.980] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001974)  
So another priority program for next year using the augmentation in budget

**[01:32:13.400] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001975)  
is to further increase the number of participants to the Privacy Awareness Week.

**[01:32:19.060] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001976)  
So through Presidential Proclamation 512,

**[01:32:21.480] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001977)  
the end of May every year is declared to be Privacy Awareness Week.

**[01:32:25.020] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001978)  
And the NPC,

**[01:32:27.320] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001979)  
holds a large forum for both local and regional participants

**[01:32:32.320] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001980)  
to discuss emerging and current issues regarding data protection and privacy.

**[01:32:37.980] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001981)  
Also a priority program next year is the conduct of an expanded national data privacy survey.

**[01:32:43.440] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001982)  
So in 2021, the NPC conducted a similar survey and it yielded a result of 25% awareness across the nation.

**[01:32:51.320] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001983)  
So we'd like to identify whether six years after, how we have moved the needle

**[01:32:56.440] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001984)  
so that we can have

**[01:32:57.360] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001985)  
further data to further inform our policy objectives moving forward.

**[01:33:04.080] NPC Privacy Commissioner Johann Carlos S. Barcena** (asr-001986)  
That's it, Madam Chair. Thank you.
